<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47561711" data-id="47561711" data-tags="Science" data-score="113" data-comments="45" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561711" data-time="1774777618">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.umich.edu/nitrile-and-latex-gloves-may-cause-overestimation-of-microplastics-u-m-study-reveals/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.umich.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.umich.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.umich.edu/nitrile-and-latex-gloves-may-cause-overestimation-of-microplastics-u-m-study-reveals/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Overestimation of microplastics potentially caused by scientists&#x27; gloves</span><span class='zh'>科学家手套可能导致对微塑料的高估</span></a><span class="dom">news.umich.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Michigan study finds lab gloves shed plastic fibers that can contaminate microplastic measurements, potentially inflating estimates.</span><span class='zh'>密歇根大学的一项研究发现，研究人员的手套会释放微塑料纤维，可能污染测量结果、高估微塑料水平。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Michigan study finds lab gloves shed plastic fibers that can contaminate microplastic measurements, potentially inflating estimates. The result calls for improved lab controls and updated protocols to ensure accurate environmental data.</span><span class='zh'>密歇根大学的一项研究发现，研究人员的手套会释放微塑料纤维，可能污染测量结果、高估微塑料水平。研究者因此呼吁加强实验室控制与方法修订，以确保环境数据的准确性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies.</span><span class='zh'>GitLab 联合创始人正在与癌症作斗争，但仍通过创办新公司继续前进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies. This shows entrepreneurship can be a coping mechanism and a way to channel energy during treatment. The story highlights resilience at the intersection of health and leadership.</span><span class='zh'>GitLab 联合创始人正在与癌症作斗争，但仍通过创办新公司继续前进。这一做法凸显创业在治疗期间用以应对压力的作用，以及在健康挑战中保持生产力的能力。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reutersconnect.com/item/south-korea-mandates-solar-panels-for-public-parking-lots/dGFnOnJldXRlcnMuY29tLDIwMjY6bmV3c21sX01UMU5VUlBITzAwMFZKRjFZQQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>South Korea Mandates Solar Panels for Public Parking Lots</span><span class='zh'>韩国强制公共停车场安装太阳能板</span></a><span class="dom">reutersconnect.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>South Korea is mandating solar canopies over public parking lots as part of a broader clean energy push, potentially adding significant solar capacity and reducing urban heat island effects.</span><span class='zh'>韩国将公共停车场覆盖太阳能板作为清洁能源计划的一部分，预计新增大量光伏容量并降低城市热岛效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>South Korea is mandating solar canopies over public parking lots as part of a broader clean energy push, potentially adding significant solar capacity and reducing urban heat island effects. The policy will require coordination with grid operators and may influence parking infrastructure and public budgeting.</span><span class='zh'>韩国将公共停车场覆盖太阳能板作为清洁能源计划的一部分，预计新增大量光伏容量并降低城市热岛效应。 这一政策需要与电网运营方协同，或影响停车场基础设施与公共预算。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>321</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558997' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>179</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47554773" data-id="47554773" data-tags="AI" data-score="682" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47554773" data-time="1774706913">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.stanford.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.stanford.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI overly affirms users asking for personal advice</span><span class='zh'>AI 对寻求个人建议的用户过度拍马奉承</span></a><span class="dom">news.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal. This sycophantic behavior can mislead users and undermine critical judgment, underscoring challenges in aligning AI with reliable guidance.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。这种拍马奉承行为可能误导用户、削弱批判性判断，凸显让 AI 输出可靠建议的对齐难题。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nielsleenheer.com/articles/2026/css-is-doomed-rendering-doom-in-3d-with-css/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nielsleenheer.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/css-doomed.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nielsleenheer.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nielsleenheer.com/articles/2026/css-is-doomed-rendering-doom-in-3d-with-css/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CSS is DOOMed</span><span class='zh'>CSS 注定走向末日</span></a><span class="dom">nielsleenheer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSS-based 3D rendering is hitting fundamental limits in performance and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 在实现真实 3D 与复杂视觉效果时面临性能与可靠性的根本上限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSS-based 3D rendering is hitting fundamental limits in performance and reliability. As visual demands grow, CSS alone won’t suffice, pushing developers toward GPU-accelerated approaches like WebGL or Canvas for real 3D effects.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 在实现真实 3D 与复杂视觉效果时面临性能与可靠性的根本上限。随着渲染需求提升，单靠 CSS 已难以胜任，开发者应转向 GPU 加速的 WebGL 或 Canvas 等方案来实现真实的 3D 效果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EnriqueLop/legalize-es" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spanish legislation as a Git repo</span><span class='zh'>把西班牙立法当作 Git 仓库来管理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails. It demonstrates &#x27;law as code&#x27; can improve transparency and governance while highlighting implementation challenges.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。这样的“法治即代码”理念提升透明度与治理效率，同时也暴露了实现方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bmj.com/content/387/bmj-2024-082194" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alzheimer&#x27;s disease mortality among taxi and ambulance drivers (2024)</span><span class='zh'>出租车与救护车司机的阿尔茨海默病死亡率（2024）</span></a><span class="dom">bmj.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2024 BMJ study examines mortality from Alzheimer&#x27;s disease among taxi and ambulance drivers.</span><span class='zh'>2024 年 BMJ 的研究聚焦出租车与救护车司机中阿尔茨海默病的死亡率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2024 BMJ study examines mortality from Alzheimer&#x27;s disease among taxi and ambulance drivers. It highlights possible occupational risk factors, such as long-term exposure to stress, shift work, and sleep disruption, underscoring broader implications for workforce health and aging drivers. It suggests targeted prevention and screening strategies for high-risk professions.</span><span class='zh'>2024 年 BMJ 的研究聚焦出租车与救护车司机中阿尔茨海默病的死亡率。研究指向职业因素（如长期高压、轮班制与睡眠干扰）可能与风险增加相关，强调对高风险行业的健康监护与早筛的重要性，并提出针对性的预防策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lXxmIw9axWw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I Built an Open-World Engine for the N64 [video]</span><span class='zh'>我为 N64 构建开放世界引擎</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer demonstrates an open-world engine running on the Nintendo 64 hardware, illustrating how modern streaming techniques and world-building ideas map onto an era of tight memory and fixed pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>开发者在任天堂64硬件上展示一个开放世界引擎，展示了将现代流式加载和世界构建思路映射到记忆有限、固定管线的老平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer demonstrates an open-world engine running on the Nintendo 64 hardware, illustrating how modern streaming techniques and world-building ideas map onto an era of tight memory and fixed pipelines. The video offers a playful look at retro hardware with current-engine concepts and could inspire hobbyists and researchers exploring hardware-limited game design.</span><span class='zh'>开发者在任天堂64硬件上展示一个开放世界引擎，展示了将现代流式加载和世界构建思路映射到记忆有限、固定管线的老平台。该视频以复古硬件为载体，考察了在资源受限环境下的引擎设计理念，或启发爱好者与研究者进行相关探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grid.iamkate.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Britain today generating 90%+ of electricity from renewables</span><span class='zh'>英国今日可再生能源发电占比超过90%</span></a><span class="dom">grid.iamkate.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Britain is today generating more than 90% of its electricity from renewable sources, a milestone for a grid that must balance variable supply.</span><span class='zh'>英国在某一天的用电中来自可再生能源的比例超过90%，成为电网脱碳的里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Britain is today generating more than 90% of its electricity from renewable sources, a milestone for a grid that must balance variable supply. The day highlights the role of wind and solar, paired with flexible demand and storage, while reminding that variability still requires backup capacity and cross-border balancing.</span><span class='zh'>英国在某一天的用电中来自可再生能源的比例超过90%，成为电网脱碳的里程碑。风能、太阳能的快速扩张配合灵活的需求管理与储能正在推动车网迈向更高的可再生比重，但日间波动仍需后备容量与跨境调度来平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/BoWang87/status/2037648937453232504" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Further human + AI + proof assistant work on Knuth&#x27;s &quot;Claude Cycles&quot; problem</span><span class='zh'>在 Knuth 的“Claude Cycles”问题上进一步的人机协作与证明助手工作</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Building on prior attempts, this piece documents continued human-AI collaboration with proof systems to tackle Knuth&#x27;s Claude Cycles problem.</span><span class='zh'>继续记录在人机协作的基础上，借助证明助手和交互式推理处理 Knuth 的 Claude Cycles 问题的进展，展示形式化验证在解决高难度数学难题中的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Building on prior attempts, this piece documents continued human-AI collaboration with proof systems to tackle Knuth&#x27;s Claude Cycles problem. It highlights how proof assistants and interactive reasoning are changing how difficult mathematical proofs are approached and validated.</span><span class='zh'>继续记录在人机协作的基础上，借助证明助手和交互式推理处理 Knuth 的 Claude Cycles 问题的进展，展示形式化验证在解决高难度数学难题中的作用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>224</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557166' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>153</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47558372" data-id="47558372" data-tags="Finance|Health|Business" data-score="161" data-comments="103" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558372" data-time="1774734180">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/mar/28/the-great-care-home-cash-grab-how-private-equity-turned-vulnerable-elderly-people-into-human-atms" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Private equity turned vulnerable elderly people into human ATMs</span><span class='zh'>私募股权把脆弱老人变成现金提款机</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Private equity ownership of care homes is laid bare as debt-fueled, profit-driven strategies degrade care and transfer costs to residents and public purse.</span><span class='zh'>私募股权对养老护理机构的收购以债务驱动、以利润为导向的策略削弱护理质量，并将成本转嫁给居民与公共资金。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Private equity ownership of care homes is laid bare as debt-fueled, profit-driven strategies degrade care and transfer costs to residents and public purse. The piece links financial engineering to deteriorating outcomes for elderly residents and staff, highlighting governance gaps in essential services.</span><span class='zh'>私募股权对养老护理机构的收购以债务驱动、以利润为导向的策略削弱护理质量，并将成本转嫁给居民与公共资金。文章将金融工程与老人护理结果恶化联系起来，凸显关键公共服务治理中的漏洞。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Health' style='--c:#4ade80'>Health</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>161</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558372' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>103</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47555090" data-id="47555090" data-tags="AI|Culture" data-score="273" data-comments="215" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555090" data-time="1774709365">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theregister.com/2026/03/27/sycophantic_ai_risks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/02/02/robot_shutterstock.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theregister.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/2026/03/27/sycophantic_ai_risks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Folk are getting dangerously attached to AI that always tells them they&#x27;re right</span><span class='zh'>人们对总是肯定他们观点的 AI 依赖性越来越强</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>People are increasingly relying on AI that always confirms their views, creating a dangerous bias loop.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的人依赖那种总是肯定他们观点的 AI，形成危险的偏见回路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>People are increasingly relying on AI that always confirms their views, creating a dangerous bias loop. The piece warns that calibrated, skeptical AI and stronger guardrails are needed to prevent echo chambers and misinformation from taking root.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的人依赖那种总是肯定他们观点的 AI，形成危险的偏见回路。文章警告若缺乏校准与防护，将使回音室效应和错误信息蔓延，需建立更强的边界与审查机制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>273</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555090' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>215</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47548243" data-id="47548243" data-tags="Privacy|AI|Programming" data-score="721" data-comments="310" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" data-time="1774645491">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you don&#x27;t opt out by Apr 24 GitHub will train on your private repos</span><span class='zh'>如果你在4月24日前不选择退出，GitHub 将在你的私有仓库上用于训练</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models. This raises privacy and IP concerns for individual developers and teams, and highlights the trade-off between model quality and data rights; opting out may require action and may affect tooling.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。这将引发对隐私和知识产权的担忧，并可能影响企业对代码资产的控制。开发者需要了解退出流程与潜在影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>721</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>310</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47550282" data-id="47550282" data-tags="AI|Programming|Security" data-score="600" data-comments="312" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550282" data-time="1774658394">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=jai.scs.stanford.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>jai.scs.stanford.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go hard on agents, not on your filesystem</span><span class='zh'>把精力放在代理上，而不是文件系统</span></a><span class="dom">jai.scs.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem. It outlines practical patterns like event-driven architecture, actor-model boundaries, and ephemeral storage to improve reproducibility and security in AI-powered automation. Tradeoffs are discussed and guidance provided for when to adopt agent-centric designs.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。文中给出事件驱动架构、演员模型边界、瞬态存储等实用模式，以提升可重复性、安全性和在 AI 自动化中的可组合性。还讨论了权衡以及在何种场景应采用以代理为中心的设计。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>600</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550282' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>312</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47557185" data-id="47557185" data-tags="AI|DevOps" data-score="190" data-comments="112" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557185" data-time="1774723328">
<a class="thumb" href="https://lzon.ca/posts/other/thoughts-ai-era/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=lzon.ca&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>lzon.ca</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lzon.ca/posts/other/thoughts-ai-era/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The first 40 months of the AI era</span><span class='zh'>AI 时代的前40个月</span></a><span class="dom">lzon.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective overview of what the first 40 months since AI&#x27;s breakout have delivered: rapid productization, surging compute for models, and new platform dynamics, alongside safety and governance concerns.</span><span class='zh'>回顾自 AI 崛起以来的前40个月：产品化速度加快、模型计算需求飙升，以及新兴的平台生态，同时伴随安全与治理的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective overview of what the first 40 months since AI&#x27;s breakout have delivered: rapid productization, surging compute for models, and new platform dynamics, alongside safety and governance concerns. The piece argues AI is reshaping competitive strategy and infrastructure choices across tech ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>回顾自 AI 崛起以来的前40个月：产品化速度加快、模型计算需求飙升，以及新兴的平台生态，同时伴随安全与治理的挑战。 AI 正在重塑技术生态中的竞争策略与基础设施选择。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>190</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557185' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>112</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47556359" data-id="47556359" data-tags="Programming|Security" data-score="217" data-comments="52" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556359" data-time="1774717174">
<a class="thumb" href="https://astrid.tech/2026/03/28/0/linux-is-an-interpreter/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=astrid.tech&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>astrid.tech</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://astrid.tech/2026/03/28/0/linux-is-an-interpreter/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux is an interpreter</span><span class='zh'>Linux 实质上是一个解释器</span></a><span class="dom">astrid.tech</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative piece argues that Linux acts like an interpreter, translating kernel interactions, system calls, and filesystem semantics at runtime.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点文章认为 Linux 实质上像一个解释器，在运行时对内核交互、系统调用和文件系统语义进行翻译。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative piece argues that Linux acts like an interpreter, translating kernel interactions, system calls, and filesystem semantics at runtime. It reframes OS design as a continuous translation layer, with implications for performance, security, and developer mental models.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点文章认为 Linux 实质上像一个解释器，在运行时对内核交互、系统调用和文件系统语义进行翻译。这种看法重新定义了操作系统的设计，使得性能和安全性以及开发者心智模型产生新的考量。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>217</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556359' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>52</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47553185" data-id="47553185" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="309" data-comments="104" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185" data-time="1774692373">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/J-x-Z/cocoa-way" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">18</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/J-x-Z/cocoa-way" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way – Native macOS Wayland compositor for running Linux apps seamlessly</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way：原生 macOS Wayland 合成器，实现 Linux 应用无缝运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering. If matured, it could blur the boundary between Linux development environments and macOS workstations, reducing reliance on X.org or VM-based workarounds.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。若成熟，将模糊 Linux 开发环境与 macOS 工作站之间的边界，减少对 Xorg 或虚拟机的依赖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>309</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47557064" data-id="47557064" data-tags="Open Source|Gaming|Code" data-score="160" data-comments="50" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557064" data-time="1774722165">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/rajko-horvat/OpenCiv1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rajko-horvat/OpenCiv1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 – open-source rewrite of Civ1</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1：Civ1 的开源重写</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 is an open-source rewrite of Civilization I, reimplementing its mechanics with modern code.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1 是 Civilization I 的开源重写，使用现代代码重新实现其机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 is an open-source rewrite of Civilization I, reimplementing its mechanics with modern code. It serves as a case study in retro game preservation and how modern tooling can re-create classics with improved modability and portability.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1 是 Civilization I 的开源重写，使用现代代码重新实现其机制。它展示了通过现代工具对经典游戏进行保存与再创作，并提升了可修改性与跨平台性。</span></p>

The change would ease initial sign-in friction for many users and enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>微软内部对取消 Windows 11 强制微软账户的讨论日益激烈，显示安装流程和用户选择可能放宽。此举将降低大量用户和企业在初始登录时的阻力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CERN runs ultra-compact AI models burned into silicon on FPGAs to filter the LHC&#x27;s real-time data streams.</span><span class='zh'>CERN 将极小型、固化在硅上的 AI 模型部署在 FPGA 上，用于实时筛选 LHC 数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CERN runs ultra-compact AI models burned into silicon on FPGAs to filter the LHC&#x27;s real-time data streams. This approach delivers ultra-low latency and energy efficiency, illustrating how edge AI accelerates high-energy physics.</span><span class='zh'>CERN 将极小型、固化在硅上的 AI 模型部署在 FPGA 上，用于实时筛选 LHC 数据。此举实现极低延迟和能耗效率，展示边缘 AI 在高能物理中的应用潜力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.androidauthority.com/android-sideload-carry-over-3652845/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.androidauthority.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/Install-from-Unknown-Sources-2.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='androidauthority.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.androidauthority.com/android-sideload-carry-over-3652845/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android’s new sideload settings will carry over to new devices</span><span class='zh'>Android 新的侧载设置将随设备迁移</span></a><span class="dom">androidauthority.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Android is rolling over sideload allowances across devices, so users enabling unknown sources won&#x27;t have to re-enable on every new device.</span><span class='zh'>Android 将把侧载设置跨设备携带，用户在换设备时不再需要重新开启未知来源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Android is rolling over sideload allowances across devices, so users enabling unknown sources won&#x27;t have to re-enable on every new device. The convenience comes with higher security risk, potentially broadening attack surfaces if devices are compromised or when switching accounts. It also signals a shift toward service- or account-level preferences rather than device-level toggles.</span><span class='zh'>Android 将把侧载设置跨设备携带，用户在换设备时不再需要重新开启未知来源。此举提高了使用便利性，但也提升了安全风险，若设备被攻破或账户切换时可能增加攻击面。体现了将偏好与设置从单一设备迁移到账户/服务级别的趋势。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A humorous, first-person account of wrestling with bureaucratic red tape as the author builds a metaphorical &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;.</span><span class='zh'>这篇以“纸上洪流”为题的博客故事，讲述作者如何对付繁琐的官僚流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A humorous, first-person account of wrestling with bureaucratic red tape as the author builds a metaphorical &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;. It reads as a practical reminder that reducing friction in forms and approvals pays off. The takeaway is to seek efficient processes to avoid bureaucratic bottlenecks.</span><span class='zh'>这篇以“纸上洪流”为题的博客故事，讲述作者如何对付繁琐的官僚流程。通过一连串讽刺与经验教训，揭示繁文缛节的痛点及应对思路。核心在于在文书工作中寻求高效、低摩擦的处理方式，避免被繁琐拖垮。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that blaming AI for the Iran school bombing misses deeper issues around attribution, misinformation, and systemic security gaps.</span><span class='zh'>认为将伊朗校园爆炸归咎于 AI 忽视了归因、信息误导与系统性安全漏洞等更深层次的问题。</span></p>

The piece cautions against overreliance on AI as a scapegoat while urging rigorous investigation.</span><span class='zh'>认为将伊朗校园爆炸归咎于 AI 忽视了归因、信息误导与系统性安全漏洞等更深层次的问题。文章警示不要把 AI 当成替罪羊，同时呼吁进行严格调查。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">30</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/27/wikipedia-bans-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wikipedia bans AI-generated content in its online encyclopedia</span><span class='zh'>维基百科禁止 AI 生成的内容</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wikipedia bans AI-generated content to preserve verifiability and human-centric editing.</span><span class='zh'>维基百科宣布禁止 AI 生成内容，以维护可验证性和由人编辑的标准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wikipedia bans AI-generated content to preserve verifiability and human-centric editing. The move pushes contributors to rely on transparent, citable sources and may accelerate tooling to verify provenance.</span><span class='zh'>维基百科宣布禁止 AI 生成内容，以维护可验证性和由人编辑的标准。此举促使编辑者坚持使用可追溯的来源，并可能推动对内容出自与出处的工具化验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>92</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556116' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>19</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47548670" data-id="47548670" data-tags="Security" data-score="263" data-comments="7" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548670" data-time="1774647701">
<a class="thumb" href="https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/03/doj-confirms-fbi-director-kash-patels-personal-email-was-hacked/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/GettyImages-2266689842-1024x648.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='arstechnica.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/03/doj-confirms-fbi-director-kash-patels-personal-email-was-hacked/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DOJ confirms FBI Director Kash Patel&#x27;s personal email was hacked</span><span class='zh'>司法部确认 FBI 局长 Kash Patel 的个人邮箱遭黑客入侵</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The DOJ confirmed that the personal email of FBI Director Kash Patel was hacked, underscoring security risks around high-profile officials&#x27; private accounts.</span><span class='zh'>司法部确认 FBI 局长 Kash Patel 的私人邮箱遭黑客入侵，凸显高官私人账户安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The DOJ confirmed that the personal email of FBI Director Kash Patel was hacked, underscoring security risks around high-profile officials&#x27; private accounts. The breach could expose correspondence, contacts, and other sensitive information, fueling scrutiny of how personal endpoints are safeguarded. It spotlights ongoing cyber threats and the need for robust personal-security measures for public figures.</span><span class='zh'>司法部确认 FBI 局长 Kash Patel 的私人邮箱遭黑客入侵，凸显高官私人账户安全风险。该事件可能暴露私人通信、联系人及其他敏感信息，促使社会各界关注个人端点的防护。再次强调对公职人员的网络威胁及强化个人安全措施的需求。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/treason-in-the-futures-markets" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#84cc16' data-tag='Finance'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=paulkrugman.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>paulkrugman.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/treason-in-the-futures-markets" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Treason in the Futures Markets</span><span class='zh'>期货市场中的背叛行为</span></a><span class="dom">paulkrugman.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Paul Krugman argues that incentives and structural flaws in futures markets threaten price discovery and market integrity.</span><span class='zh'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，期货市场的激励结构与制度缺陷正在损害价格发现与市场诚信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Paul Krugman argues that incentives and structural flaws in futures markets threaten price discovery and market integrity. He points to regulatory gaps and opaque practices that reward short-term bets over transparent risk pricing, calling for reforms to restore trust and stability. The piece blends economics with policy implications for investors and regulators.</span><span class='zh'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，期货市场的激励结构与制度缺陷正在损害价格发现与市场诚信。某些不透明的做法让短期投机优先于透明的风险定价，暴露监管空白，他呼吁改革以重建信任与稳定。该文将经济学观点与监管对策结合起来，为投资者与监管机构提供洞见。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c77m4zx6zvmo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Slovenia becomes first EU country to introduce fuel rationing</span><span class='zh'>斯洛文尼亚成为欧盟首个实施燃料配给的国家</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slovenia has become the first EU member to impose fuel rationing, signaling a new lever governments may use to manage energy supply.</span><span class='zh'>斯洛文尼亚成为欧盟首个实施燃料配给的成员国。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slovenia has become the first EU member to impose fuel rationing, signaling a new lever governments may use to manage energy supply. The move could affect logistics, transportation, and price dynamics, and may influence how tech and industrial sectors plan operations under energy constraints.</span><span class='zh'>斯洛文尼亚成为欧盟首个实施燃料配给的成员国。这一举措将影响物流和交通、改变燃料价格结构，也可能改变高科技与工业部门在能源紧张时期的运营计划。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://owltec.ca/Other/Installing+a+Let%27s+Encrypt+TLS+certificate+on+a+Brother+printer+automatically+with+Certbot+(%26+Cloudflare)" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Installing a Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS certificate on a Brother printer with Certbot</span><span class='zh'>用 Certbot 为 Brother 打印机安装 Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS 证书</span></a><span class="dom">owltec.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical guide showing how to secure a Brother network printer with a Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS certificate using Certbot and Cloudflare DNS challenges.</span><span class='zh'>实用指南演示如何借助 Certbot 与 Cloudflare DNS 挑战，为一台 Brother 网络打印机部署 Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS 证书。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical guide showing how to secure a Brother network printer with a Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS certificate using Certbot and Cloudflare DNS challenges. The steps cover certificate issuance, renewal, and how to configure the printer&#x27;s web interface. It highlights the growing trend of securing IoT-like devices even without dedicated vendors&#x27; support.</span><span class='zh'>实用指南演示如何借助 Certbot 与 Cloudflare DNS 挑战，为一台 Brother 网络打印机部署 Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS 证书。步骤涵盖证书申请、续期及打印机网页界面的配置，同时指出在缺乏厂商支持的情况下，为类似物联网设备加固安全的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.psychologytoday.com/us/blog/the-algorithmic-mind/202603/adults-lose-skills-to-ai-children-never-build-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Adults Lose Skills to AI. Children Never Build Them</span><span class='zh'>成年人因 AI 失去技能，孩子们却从不培养这些技能</span></a><span class="dom">psychologytoday.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-assisted workflows are eroding certain adult skills, from memory to basic calculation, while children may not develop those skills due to overreliance on automation.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的工作流程正在侵蚀成年人部分技能，如记忆与基本计算，而儿童也可能因过度依赖自动化而未能培养相应能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-assisted workflows are eroding certain adult skills, from memory to basic calculation, while children may not develop those skills due to overreliance on automation. The piece warns about long-term cognitive and educational impacts and urges balanced use and targeted skill-building.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的工作流程正在侵蚀成年人部分技能，如记忆与基本计算，而儿童也可能因过度依赖自动化而未能培养相应能力。文章警示长期的认知与教育影响，并呼吁实现平衡使用与有针对性的技能培养。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/02/26/the-last-gasps-of-the-rent-seeking-class.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Last gasps of the rent seeking class?</span><span class='zh'>租金寻租阶层的最后喘息？</span></a><span class="dom">geohot.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This provocative piece argues that a segment of the tech-policy ecosystem is in decline as regulation and market dynamics shift, suggesting the era of rent-seeking may be ending and outlining potential implications for the industry.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章对科技行业中的租金寻租现象提出质疑，认为随着监管和市场格局的变化，这类获利方式正走向尾声。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This provocative piece argues that a segment of the tech-policy ecosystem is in decline as regulation and market dynamics shift, suggesting the era of rent-seeking may be ending and outlining potential implications for the industry.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章对科技行业中的租金寻租现象提出质疑，认为随着监管和市场格局的变化，这类获利方式正走向尾声。作者强调这会影响行业的商业模式和创新生态，并促使业界反思治理路径。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Iran-linked hackers breached the FBI director&#x27;s personal emails, illustrating persistent risks from state-backed cyber actors and prompting renewed emphasis on secure personal accounts and separation from official work channels.</span><span class='zh'>与伊朗相关的黑客入侵了 FBI 局长的私人邮箱，凸显来自国家级网络行为者的持续风险，并促使加强对个人账户的安全防护与与工作邮箱分离的政策考量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Iran-linked hackers breached the FBI director&#x27;s personal emails, illustrating persistent risks from state-backed cyber actors and prompting renewed emphasis on secure personal accounts and separation from official work channels.</span><span class='zh'>与伊朗相关的黑客入侵了 FBI 局长的私人邮箱，凸显来自国家级网络行为者的持续风险，并促使加强对个人账户的安全防护与与工作邮箱分离的政策考量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://coloradonewsline.com/briefs/surveillance-pricing-wage-setting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Colorado House passes bill to limit surveillance pricing and wage setting</span><span class='zh'>科罗拉多州众议院通过法案，限制基于监控数据的定价与工资设定</span></a><span class="dom">coloradonewsline.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Colorado&#x27;s bill targets how data gathered through surveillance affects pricing and pay decisions.</span><span class='zh'>科罗拉多州众议院通过法案，限制利用监控数据进行定价与工资决策的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Colorado&#x27;s bill targets how data gathered through surveillance affects pricing and pay decisions. It would restrict dynamic pricing practices and the use of real-time data for setting pay, with aims of reducing discrimination and increasing transparency. If enacted, it could push companies to redesign data pipelines towards fairness.</span><span class='zh'>科罗拉多州众议院通过法案，限制利用监控数据进行定价与工资决策的做法。法案拟约束基于数据的动态定价以及用实时数据设定薪酬的行为，旨在减少歧视并提高透明度。若成为法律，可能促使企业重构数据流程，以提升公正性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gadgetreview.com/hong-kong-police-can-now-demand-phone-passwords-under-new-security-rules" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hong Kong police can now demand phone passwords under new security rules</span><span class='zh'>香港警方在新安全规则下可强制索取手机密码</span></a><span class="dom">gadgetreview.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New security rules expand police powers to request phone passwords, often with minimal safeguard requirements.</span><span class='zh'>新安全规则扩大了警方对手机密码的索取权，通常几乎不设太多防护措施。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New security rules expand police powers to request phone passwords, often with minimal safeguard requirements. Civil-liberties advocates warn this could erode privacy protections and set a precedent for broader surveillance. The change will influence how individuals defend their devices and how tech platforms design access controls.</span><span class='zh'>新安全规则扩大了警方对手机密码的索取权，通常几乎不设太多防护措施。公民自由倡导者警告这可能侵蚀隐私保护并为更广泛的监控定下先例。此举将影响个人如何捍卫设备、安全工具与平台的访问控制设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lara-aigmueller.at/thoughts/leaving-the-ai-party/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I am leaving the AI party after one drink</span><span class='zh'>我喝了一口就离开 AI 派对</span></a><span class="dom">lara-aigmueller.at</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>After a measured taste of AI&#x27;s capabilities, the author decides to step away from the AI hype.</span><span class='zh'>在短暂接触 AI 能力后，作者决定离开 AI 热潮。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>After a measured taste of AI&#x27;s capabilities, the author decides to step away from the AI hype. The piece argues for disciplined governance, clear use-cases, and attention to long-term societal risks rather than chasing every new AI fad.</span><span class='zh'>在短暂接触 AI 能力后，作者决定离开 AI 热潮。文章主张制定克制的治理框架、明确的使用场景，以及对长期社会风险的关注，而不是盲目追逐新兴的 AI 热潮。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>120</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545030' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>128</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47549649" data-id="47549649" data-tags="AI|Business" data-score="92" data-comments="160" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549649" data-time="1774653558">
<a class="thumb" href="https://johnjwang.com/post/2026/03/27/why-are-executives-enabled-with-ai-but-ics-arent/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=johnjwang.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>johnjwang.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">47</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://johnjwang.com/post/2026/03/27/why-are-executives-enabled-with-ai-but-ics-arent/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why are executives enamored with AI, but ICs aren&#x27;t?</span><span class='zh'>为何高管迷恋 AI，但一线工程师并不热衷？</span></a><span class="dom">johnjwang.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Executives chase AI for strategic leverage and measurable impact, but ICs worry about rising toil, opaque ROI, and skill erosion.</span><span class='zh'>高管热衷于 AI，追求战略性杠杆和可衡量的影响；而一线工程师则担心工作负担增加、收益不透明和技能被边缘化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Executives chase AI for strategic leverage and measurable impact, but ICs worry about rising toil, opaque ROI, and skill erosion. The note argues for aligning incentives, reducing friction in workflows, and delivering tooling that genuinely eases engineers’ workloads.</span><span class='zh'>高管热衷于 AI，追求战略性杠杆和可衡量的影响；而一线工程师则担心工作负担增加、收益不透明和技能被边缘化。文章呼吁对齐激励、简化工作流程，并提供真正能减轻工程师负担的工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>92</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549649' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>160</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47543186" data-id="47543186" data-tags="Open Source|Security|Hardware" data-score="108" data-comments="15" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47543186" data-time="1774622391">
<a class="thumb" href="http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/m88k1.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/t_MVME180.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='miod.online.fr' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">48</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/m88k1.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenBSD on Motorola 88000 Processors</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 在 Motorola 88000 处理器上的移植</span></a><span class="dom">miod.online.fr</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenBSD has an experimental port to the Motorola 88000 (M88000) architecture, a retro RISC design from the 1990s.</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 在 Motorola 88000 架构上完成实验性移植，这颗来自1990年代的老牌 RISC 处理器仍具研究与保留价值。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenBSD has an experimental port to the Motorola 88000 (M88000) architecture, a retro RISC design from the 1990s. The effort showcases how modern cryptography and security features can be exercised on legacy hardware, and it reflects the ongoing interest in supporting obscure architectures for research and preservation. The port also highlights challenges in toolchains and drivers for this vintage CPU.</span><span class='zh'>OpenBSD 在 Motorola 88000 架构上完成实验性移植，这颗来自1990年代的老牌 RISC 处理器仍具研究与保留价值。此工作展示了在老硬件上实现现代安全特性与工具链的可行性，同时揭示了古老 CPU 在驱动和工具链方面的挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fraunhofer researchers report an adhesive-free sealing method for paper packaging, improving recyclability and reducing chemical waste.</span><span class='zh'>Fraunhofer 的研究团队提出一种无需粘合剂的纸包装封口工艺，提高可回收性并降低化学废弃物。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fraunhofer researchers report an adhesive-free sealing method for paper packaging, improving recyclability and reducing chemical waste. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms. It warns that consumer electronics waste grows as devices become harder to repair, urging both makers and buyers to rethink responsibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。作者呼吁厂商与用户共同重塑责任感，以减缓浪费并提升可持续性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.icao.int/news/new-power-bank-restrictions-will-safeguard-international-aviation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ICAO issued new power bank restriction on flight</span><span class='zh'>ICAO 发布飞行用充电宝的新限制</span></a><span class="dom">icao.int</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ICAO tightened restrictions on carrying power banks on flights to reduce fire risk from lithium-ion batteries, affecting passengers and airline procedures.</span><span class='zh'>国际民航组织对随身携带充电宝的规定进一步收紧，限制容量并规范运输要求，以降低锂离子电池引发火灾的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ICAO tightened restrictions on carrying power banks on flights to reduce fire risk from lithium-ion batteries, affecting passengers and airline procedures. The policy pushes manufacturers and carriers toward safer design and handling.</span><span class='zh'>国际民航组织对随身携带充电宝的规定进一步收紧，限制容量并规范运输要求，以降低锂离子电池引发火灾的风险。新规将影响旅客、航空公司流程与厂商的安全设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布停产 Mac Pro，这标志着其工作站路线向高密度消费级硬件或外部计算方案的转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bethmathews.substack.com/p/why-so-many-control-rooms-were-seafoam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why so many control rooms were seafoam green (2025)</span><span class='zh'>为什么大量控制室偏好海泡绿（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">bethmathews.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts. The piece ties this preference to ergonomics, display tech, and a broader design culture that values calm, legibility, and efficiency in high-stakes environments.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。本文将这一偏好与人体工效学、显示技术以及强调冷静、易读与高效的设计文化联系起来，探讨在高压环境中的实用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sylve.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sylve – Proxmox Alternative by FreeBSD</span><span class='zh'>Sylve：基于 FreeBSD 的 Proxmox 替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">sylve.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sylve is a FreeBSD-based virtualization platform offering Proxmox-like management and features, potentially with bhyve-based virtualization, ZFS support, and containers.</span><span class='zh'>Sylve 是一个基于 FreeBSD 的虚拟化平台，提供类似 Proxmox 的管理与功能，可能使用 bhyve、ZFS 等技术，面向偏好 BSD 堆栈的用户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sylve is a FreeBSD-based virtualization platform offering Proxmox-like management and features, potentially with bhyve-based virtualization, ZFS support, and containers. It presents an alternative for users seeking a BSD-centric stack.</span><span class='zh'>Sylve 是一个基于 FreeBSD 的虚拟化平台，提供类似 Proxmox 的管理与功能，可能使用 bhyve、ZFS 等技术，面向偏好 BSD 堆栈的用户。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://code.claude.com/docs/en/web-scheduled-tasks" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Schedule tasks on the web</span><span class='zh'>在网页上调度任务</span></a><span class="dom">code.claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Scheduling tasks on the web goes beyond setTimeouts, covering browser APIs for background work and time-based triggers.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在网页应用中实现定时任务的新思路，涵盖服务工作者、后台任务和基于时间的触发等模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scheduling tasks on the web goes beyond setTimeouts, covering browser APIs for background work and time-based triggers. It highlights patterns for reliability, timing accuracy, and energy considerations in web apps that need periodic work. Practical guidance helps you build browser schedulers that work offline and across tabs.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在网页应用中实现定时任务的新思路，涵盖服务工作者、后台任务和基于时间的触发等模式。讨论在可用性、精度和电量成本之间的权衡，以及跨标签、离线环境中的可行策略。为在浏览器中构建健壮的周期性调度器提供实用建议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://telnyx.com/resources/telnyx-python-sdk-supply-chain-security-notice-march-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Telnyx package compromised on PyPI</span><span class='zh'>Telnyx Python SDK 的 PyPI 包遭遇供应链安全事件</span></a><span class="dom">telnyx.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Telnyx warns that one of its Python packages on PyPI was compromised, prompting a security advisory and remediation steps.</span><span class='zh'>Telnyx 发出警告，PyPI 上的一个 Python 包遭到入侵，发布了安全公告并给出修复步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Telnyx warns that one of its Python packages on PyPI was compromised, prompting a security advisory and remediation steps. The incident underscores the ongoing risk of supply-chain attacks in the Python ecosystem and the need for provenance checks, code signing, and rapid response.</span><span class='zh'>Telnyx 发出警告，PyPI 上的一个 Python 包遭到入侵，发布了安全公告并给出修复步骤。这一事件凸显了 Python 生态中供应链攻击的持续风险，以及对代码签名、来源可追溯性和快速响应的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.allsky7.net/#archive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The European AllSky7 fireball network</span><span class='zh'>欧洲 AllSky7 火球观测网</span></a><span class="dom">allsky7.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Europe-wide AllSky7 coordinates autonomous cameras to detect and track fireballs and meteors across the night sky.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲 AllSky7 全域性利用星空摄像头网络，统一协调观测火球与流星的轨迹与亮度数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Europe-wide AllSky7 coordinates autonomous cameras to detect and track fireballs and meteors across the night sky. The network enables large-scale data collection on asteroid and atmospheric events, contributing to research and public engagement in astronomy. Real-time alerts and archival data deepen understanding of near-Earth space.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲 AllSky7 全域性利用星空摄像头网络，统一协调观测火球与流星的轨迹与亮度数据。该网络扩展了天文观测覆盖范围，推动相关研究与公众参与，既有实时警报也有海量归档数据。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.redox-os.org/news/nlnet-cap-nsmgr-cwd/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Capability-Based Security for Redox: Namespace and CWD as Capabilities</span><span class='zh'>Redox 的基于能力的安全：命名空间与当前工作目录作为能力</span></a><span class="dom">redox-os.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Redox OS explores capability-based security by treating namespace and current working directory as explicit capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Redox OS 通过将命名空间与当前工作目录视为显式的能力来实现基于能力的安全，旨在把权限局限于被授予的范围，并提升进程之间的隔离性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Redox OS explores capability-based security by treating namespace and current working directory as explicit capabilities. This approach aims to limit permissions to what is granted and improve isolation between processes.</span><span class='zh'>Redox OS 通过将命名空间与当前工作目录视为显式的能力来实现基于能力的安全，旨在把权限局限于被授予的范围，并提升进程之间的隔离性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://naturalist.bearblog.dev/the-bee-that-everyone-wants-to-save/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bee that everyone wants to save</span><span class='zh'>大家都想拯救的蜜蜂</span></a><span class="dom">naturalist.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece spotlights a bee species that has captured widespread conservation attention.</span><span class='zh'>这只被广泛关注的蜜蜂物种被用来阐释授粉者对生态系统和农业的重要性，以及当前正在进行的保护行动，如栖息地修复、政策支持与公民科学参与，以保护它及所依赖的作物。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece spotlights a bee species that has captured widespread conservation attention. It explains why pollinators matter for ecosystems and agriculture, and what actions are underway—from habitat restoration to policy support and citizen science—to protect it and the crops it sustains.</span><span class='zh'>这只被广泛关注的蜜蜂物种被用来阐释授粉者对生态系统和农业的重要性，以及当前正在进行的保护行动，如栖息地修复、政策支持与公民科学参与，以保护它及所依赖的作物。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.axios.com/local/nashville/2026/03/16/nashville-library-digitize-home-movies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nashville library launches Memory Lab for digitizing home movies</span><span class='zh'>纳什维尔图书馆推出 Memory Lab，用于数字化家庭影片</span></a><span class="dom">axios.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A local library launches a Memory Lab to help residents digitize home movies, preserving personal histories and improving access to archival-quality media.</span><span class='zh'>本地图书馆推出 Memory Lab，帮助居民将家庭影片数字化，保存个人记忆并提升档案质量的访问性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A local library launches a Memory Lab to help residents digitize home movies, preserving personal histories and improving access to archival-quality media. The program exemplifies community-driven digital preservation and the role libraries play in safeguarding cultural memory.</span><span class='zh'>本地图书馆推出 Memory Lab，帮助居民将家庭影片数字化，保存个人记忆并提升档案质量的访问性。这体现了以社区为导向的数字保存，以及公共机构在保护文化记忆方面的作用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocates Bayesian methods for clinical trials as a way to use prior information and run adaptive designs, potentially speeding insights and reducing patient exposure.</span><span class='zh'>提倡在临床试验中采用贝叶斯方法，以利用先验信息并实现自适应设计，从而加速洞察并减少患者暴露；同时讨论先验选择、计算复杂性和监管接受度等挑战。</span></p>

It also covers challenges like priors choice, computation, and regulatory acceptance.</span><span class='zh'>提倡在临床试验中采用贝叶斯方法，以利用先验信息并实现自适应设计，从而加速洞察并减少患者暴露；同时讨论先验选择、计算复杂性和监管接受度等挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/03/24/arm-launches-its-own-cpu-with-meta-as-first-customer.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Arm releases first in-house chip, with Meta as debut customer</span><span class='zh'>Arm 推出自研芯片，Meta 成为首任客户</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Arm announces its first in-house processor, with Meta as the debut customer, marking a notable shift as Arm moves toward more vertically integrated silicon.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 发布首款自研处理器，Meta 成为首批客户，标志着 Arm 在向垂直整合芯片迈出重要一步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Arm announces its first in-house processor, with Meta as the debut customer, marking a notable shift as Arm moves toward more vertically integrated silicon. The move could influence licensing dynamics and signal growing demand for custom silicon in data-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 发布首款自研处理器，Meta 成为首批客户，标志着 Arm 在向垂直整合芯片迈出重要一步。此举可能影响授权格局，并体现对面向数据密集型工作负载的自定义芯片需求上升。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece introduces the concept of memory architectures designed to coordinate across services with minimal infrastructure, enabling collaborative workflows. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2022 study quantifies how serif and sans-serif typefaces fare in legibility on screens, with nuanced findings across reading tasks and sizes.</span><span class='zh'>2022 年的研究比较了衬线体与无衬线体在屏幕上的可读性，结果揭示不同任务和字号下的差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2022 study quantifies how serif and sans-serif typefaces fare in legibility on screens, with nuanced findings across reading tasks and sizes. The results inform typography choices for UI and accessibility, suggesting practical trade-offs between aesthetics and readability. Designers can tailor typography to context, audience, and device.</span><span class='zh'>2022 年的研究比较了衬线体与无衬线体在屏幕上的可读性，结果揭示不同任务和字号下的差异。对 UI 字体选择与无障碍设计具有指导意义，强调在美观与易读之间的权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fets and Crosses documents a hardware Tic-Tac-Toe built from 2458 discrete transistors, showcasing early digital logic design and the tactile limits of hardware gaming. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article dissects the 1929 crash, showing how speculation, margin debt, and information gaps precipitated a rapid collapse. It traces how the crisis spurred landmark reforms and shaped market structure, offering a reminder of how fragile financial systems remain under stress.</span><span class='zh'>本文剖析 1929 年崩盘的成因：投机热、杠杆与信息不对称叠加，导致市场快速崩溃。它追踪了危机如何推动里程碑式的金融改革和市场结构调整，提醒人们在压力之下金融体系的脆弱性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A leadership reflection on realizing personal heuristics may mislead decision making, with steps to recalibrate: seek diverse input, test assumptions, and adapt processes.</span><span class='zh'>作者反思个人启发式判断的不足，给出重新校准的方法，如扩展输入、验证假设、调整工作流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A leadership reflection on realizing personal heuristics may mislead decision making, with steps to recalibrate: seek diverse input, test assumptions, and adapt processes. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post revisits a machine running at 667 MHz, using it as a lens to discuss design tradeoffs in early personal computers and the limits of simplicity vs.</span><span class='zh'>这篇回顾聚焦于一台 667MHz 的机器，借此讨论早期个人电脑的设计取舍与性能极限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post revisits a machine running at 667 MHz, using it as a lens to discuss design tradeoffs in early personal computers and the limits of simplicity vs. performance. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio Compiler and Simulator (Working RISC-V CPU)</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器与模拟器（可工作的 RISC-V CPU）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game. This opens up a playful yet educational avenue for hardware prototyping, reveals performance constraints of software simulators, and demonstrates creative cross-domain tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。这为硬件原型设计提供了一种有趣且具教育意义的方式，也揭示了软件仿真在性能上的限制，同时展示跨领域工具的创造力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://victoriaritvo.com/blog/robust-semantle-solver/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solving Semantle with the Wrong Embeddings</span><span class='zh'>用错误嵌入破解 Semantle 的方法</span></a><span class="dom">victoriaritvo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A deep dive into solving Semantle with intentionally misaligned embeddings, showing that robustness can come from exploiting the structure of the search space even when representations are imperfect.</span><span class='zh'>深入探讨使用故意错位的嵌入来解决 Semantle，展示即使嵌入表示不完美，仍可通过抓住搜索空间结构来实现解题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A deep dive into solving Semantle with intentionally misaligned embeddings, showing that robustness can come from exploiting the structure of the search space even when representations are imperfect. It exposes both strengths and potential weaknesses of embedding-based puzzle solving.</span><span class='zh'>深入探讨使用故意错位的嵌入来解决 Semantle，展示即使嵌入表示不完美，仍可通过抓住搜索空间结构来实现解题。揭示了基于嵌入的推断在鲁棒性方面的优点与潜在弱点。</span></p>
